    11→use crate::tspec::{expand_target_dir, load_spec, spec_name_from_path};
    12→use crate::types::{Spec, flatten_config};
    13→
    14→const TSPEC_BUILD_RS_MARKER: &str = "// Generated by tspec - do not edit";
    15→
    16→/// Built-in cargo profiles that don't require a `[profile.<name>]` definition.
    17→const BUILTIN_PROFILES: &[&str] = &["dev", "debug", "release", "test", "bench"];
    18→
    19→/// Validate that a custom profile is defined in the workspace Cargo.toml.
    20→/// Built-in profiles (dev, release, test, bench) are always valid.
    21→/// Custom profiles must have a `[profile.<name>]` section with `inherits`.
    22→pub fn validate_profile(profile: &str, workspace: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    23→    if BUILTIN_PROFILES.contains(&profile) {
    24→        return Ok(());
    25→    }
    26→    let cargo_toml_path = workspace.join("Cargo.toml");
    27→    let content = fs::read_to_string(&cargo_toml_path)
    28→        .with_context(|| format!("failed to read {}", cargo_toml_path.display()))?;
    29→    let doc: toml::Value =
    30→        toml::from_str(&content).context("failed to parse workspace Cargo.toml")?;
    31→    if let Some(profiles) = doc.get("profile").and_then(|p| p.as_table())
    32→        && profiles.contains_key(profile)
    33→    {
    34→        return Ok(());
    35→    }
    36→    bail!(
    37→        "profile `{}` is not defined in {}. Custom profiles must be defined with \
    38→         `[profile.{}]` and an `inherits` field (e.g., `inherits = \"release\"`).",
    39→        profile,
    40→        cargo_toml_path.display(),
    41→        profile,
    42→    )
    43→}
    44→/// Check for spec settings that are likely misconfigurations.
    45→/// Returns a list of warning messages (printed at top and bottom of output).
    46→pub fn check_spec_misconfigurations(pkg_name: &str, spec: &Spec, pkg_dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    47→    let mut warnings = Vec::new();
    48→    let has_linker_args = !spec.linker.args.is_empty();
    49→    let has_bin_target = pkg_dir.join("src/main.rs").exists();
    50→
    51→    // -static with non-musl target (glibc + -static segfaults)
    52→    if has_linker_args {
    53→        let has_static = spec.linker.args.iter().any(|a| a == "-static");
    54→        let is_musl = spec
    55→            .cargo
    56→            .target_triple
    57→            .as_ref()
    58→            .is_some_and(|t| t.contains("musl"));
    59→        if has_static && !is_musl {
    60→            warnings.push(format!(
    61→                "Warning: -static linker arg without musl target for {}. \
    62→                 glibc + -static often segfaults; consider using a musl target triple.",
    63→                pkg_name
    64→            ));
    65→        }
    66→    }
    67→
    68→    // linker.args on lib-only package
    69→    if has_linker_args && !has_bin_target {
    70→        warnings.push(format!(
    71→            "Warning: linker.args ignored for {} (no binary target)",
    72→            pkg_name
    73→        ));
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    warnings
    77→}
    78→
    79→/// Print collected warnings again so they're visible after cargo output.
    80→pub fn reprint_warnings(warnings: &[String]) {
    81→    for w in warnings {
    82→        println!("{}", w);
    83→    }
    84→}
    85→
    86→pub fn warn_stale_build_rs(had_stale: bool) {
    87→    if had_stale {
    88→        println!("Warning: a stale build.rs was deleted, likely from a previous interrupted build");
    89→    }
    90→}
    91→
    92→/// Check if a build.rs file was generated by tspec.
    93→/// Matches either the marker comment or a file that only contains
    94→/// `cargo:rustc-link-arg-bin=` println lines (marker-less variant).
    95→fn is_tspec_generated_build_rs(content: &str) -> bool {
    96→    if content.starts_with(TSPEC_BUILD_RS_MARKER) {
    97→        return true;
    98→    }
    99→    // Fallback: file only contains fn main, cargo:rustc-link-arg-bin printlns, and braces
   100→    let dominated_by_link_args = content
   101→        .lines()
   102→        .map(|l| l.trim())
   103→        .filter(|l| !l.is_empty())
   104→        .all(|l| {
   105→            l == "fn main() {"
   106→                || l == "}"
   107→                || l.starts_with(TSPEC_BUILD_RS_MARKER)
   108→                || l.contains("cargo:rustc-link-arg-bin=")
   109→        });
   110→    dominated_by_link_args && content.contains("cargo:rustc-link-arg-bin=")
   111→}
   112→
   113→/// Remove a stale tspec-generated build.rs left behind by an interrupted build.
   114→/// Returns true if a stale file was removed. Caller should print a warning after the build.
   115→pub fn remove_stale_tspec_build_rs(pkg_dir: &Path) -> bool {
   116→    let build_rs = pkg_dir.join("build.rs");
   117→    if let Ok(content) = fs::read_to_string(&build_rs)
   118→        && is_tspec_generated_build_rs(&content)
   119→    {
   120→        let _ = fs::remove_file(&build_rs);
   121→        return true;
   122→    }
   123→    false
   124→}
   125→
   126→/// Result of a successful build
   127→pub struct BuildResult {
   128→    pub binary_path: PathBuf,
   129→    /// The effective target directory base (e.g., "target/foo" if target_dir was set)
   130→    pub target_base: PathBuf,
   131→}
   132→
   133→/// Unified cargo runner for build and test operations.
   134→///
   135→/// Handles spec loading, build.rs generation, command construction, and cleanup.
   136→/// `cli_profile` is the profile from the CLI (e.g., "release", "release-small").
   137→/// `None` means debug (default). `Some("release")` is equivalent to `--release`.
   138→pub fn run_cargo(
   139→    mode: CargoMode,
   140→    pkg_name: &str,
   141→    tspec: Option<&str>,
   142→    cli_profile: Option<&str>,
   143→) -> Result<BuildResult> {
   144→    let workspace = find_project_root()?;
   145→    let pkg_dir = find_package_dir(&workspace, pkg_name)?;
   146→    let tspec_path = find_tspec(&pkg_dir, tspec)?;
   147→
   148→    // Resolve actual package name from Cargo.toml (needed when pkg_name is a path)
   149→    let pkg_name = get_package_name(&pkg_dir)?;
   150→
   151→    // Clean up any stale tspec-generated build.rs from interrupted builds
   152→    let had_stale_build_rs = remove_stale_tspec_build_rs(&pkg_dir);
   153→
   154→    // Track if we generated a build.rs
   155→    let build_rs_path = pkg_dir.join("build.rs");
   156→    let had_build_rs = build_rs_path.exists();
   157→
   158→    // Load spec once (if present) and compute target_dir
   159→    let (spec, expanded_td) = if let Some(path) = &tspec_path {
   160→        let s = load_spec(path)?;
   161→        let name = spec_name_from_path(path);
   162→        let td = expand_target_dir(&s, &name)?;
   163→        (Some(s), td)
   164→    } else {
   165→        (None, None)
   166→    };
   167→
   168→    // Compute paths
   169→    let target_base = match &expanded_td {
   170→        Some(td) => workspace.join("target").join(td),
   171→        None => workspace.join("target"),
   172→    };
   173→    let binary_path = if let Some(spec) = &spec {
   174→        get_binary_path(
   175→            &workspace,
   176→            &pkg_name,
   177→            spec,
   178→            cli_profile,
   179→            expanded_td.as_deref(),
   180→        )
   181→    } else {
   182→        get_binary_path_simple(&workspace, &pkg_name, cli_profile)
   183→    };
   184→
   185→    // Validate the effective profile exists before invoking cargo
   186→    let effective_profile = spec
   187→        .as_ref()
   188→        .and_then(|s| s.cargo.profile.as_deref())
   189→        .or(cli_profile);
   190→    if let Some(profile) = effective_profile {
   191→        validate_profile(profile, &workspace)?;
   192→    }
   193→
   194→    // Check for misconfigurations before running cargo
   195→    let spec_warnings = if let Some(spec) = &spec {
   196→        check_spec_misconfigurations(&pkg_name, spec, &pkg_dir)
   197→    } else {
   198→        Vec::new()
   199→    };
   200→
   201→    let verb = match mode {
   202→        CargoMode::Build => "Building",
   203→        CargoMode::Test => "Testing",
   204→    };
   205→
   206→    // Apply spec if present, otherwise plain cargo subcommand
   207→    let status = if let Some(spec) = &spec {
   208→        let path = tspec_path.as_ref().unwrap();
   209→        println!("{} {} with spec {}", verb, pkg_name, path.display());
   210→
   211→        // Generate temporary build.rs for linker flags if needed
   212→        let has_linker_args = !spec.linker.args.is_empty();
   213→        let has_bin_target = pkg_dir.join("src/main.rs").exists();
   214→        if has_linker_args && has_bin_target && !had_build_rs {
   215→            generate_build_rs(&build_rs_path, &pkg_name, spec)?;
   216→        }
   217→
   218→        let mut cmd = build_cargo_command(spec, mode)?;
   219→        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());
   220→        cmd.arg("-p").arg(&pkg_name);
   221→        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);
   222→
   223→        // Set spec path for tspec-build library to read in build.rs
   224→        cmd.env("TSPEC_SPEC_FILE", path.as_os_str());
   225→
   226→        apply_spec_to_command(
   227→            &mut cmd,
   228→            spec,
   229→            &workspace,
   230→            cli_profile,
   231→            expanded_td.as_deref(),
   232→        )?;
   233→
   234→        // For test mode, append -Zpanic_abort_tests to RUSTFLAGS if needed
   235→        if mode == CargoMode::Test && needs_panic_abort_tests(spec) {
   236→            let existing = cmd
   237→                .get_envs()
   238→                .find(|(k, _)| k == &"RUSTFLAGS")
   239→                .and_then(|(_, v)| v)
   240→                .map(|v| v.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
   241→                .unwrap_or_default();
   242→            let new_flags = if existing.is_empty() {
   243→                "-Zpanic_abort_tests".to_string()
   244→            } else {
   245→                format!("{} -Zpanic_abort_tests", existing)
   246→            };
   247→            cmd.env("RUSTFLAGS", new_flags);
   248→        }
   249→
   250→        cmd.status()
   251→            .with_context(|| format!("failed to run cargo {}", mode.subcommand()))?
   252→    } else {
   253→        println!("{} {} (no tspec)", verb, pkg_name);
   254→        let mut cmd = Command::new("cargo");
   255→        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());
   256→        cmd.arg("-p").arg(&pkg_name);
   257→        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);
   258→        if let Some(p) = cli_profile {
   259→            match p {
   260→                "debug" | "dev" => {}
   261→                _ => {
   262→                    cmd.arg("--profile").arg(p);
   263→                }
   264→            }
   265→        }
   266→        cmd.status()
   267→            .with_context(|| format!("failed to run cargo {}", mode.subcommand()))?
   268→    };
   269→
   270→    // Clean up generated build.rs (only if we created it)
   271→    if !had_build_rs && build_rs_path.exists() {
   272→        let _ = fs::remove_file(&build_rs_path);
   273→    }
   274→
   275→    if !status.success() {
   276→        match &tspec_path {
   277→            Some(path) => {
   278→                let display_path = path.strip_prefix(&workspace).unwrap_or(path).display();
   279→                bail!(
   280→                    "cargo {} failed for `{}` with spec {}",
   281→                    mode.subcommand(),
   282→                    pkg_name,
   283→                    display_path
   284→                )
   285→            }
   286→            None => bail!("cargo {} failed for `{}`", mode.subcommand(), pkg_name),
   287→        }
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    if mode == CargoMode::Build {
   291→        println!("  {}", binary_path.display());
   292→    }
   293→    warn_stale_build_rs(had_stale_build_rs);
   294→    reprint_warnings(&spec_warnings);
   295→    Ok(BuildResult {
   296→        binary_path,
   297→        target_base,
   298→    })
   299→}
   300→
   301→/// Build a package with a spec, returns the binary path on success.
   302→pub fn build_package(
   303→    pkg_name: &str,
   304→    tspec: Option<&str>,
   305→    cli_profile: Option<&str>,
   306→) -> Result<BuildResult> {
   307→    run_cargo(CargoMode::Build, pkg_name, tspec, cli_profile)
   308→}
   309→
   310→/// Test a package with a spec.
   311→pub fn test_package(pkg_name: &str, tspec: Option<&str>, cli_profile: Option<&str>) -> Result<()> {
   312→    run_cargo(CargoMode::Test, pkg_name, tspec, cli_profile)?;
   313→    Ok(())
   314→}
   315→
   316→/// Plain `cargo build --release` with no spec lookup.
   317→/// Used by compare to produce a baseline build.
   318→pub fn plain_cargo_build_release(pkg_name: &str) -> Result<BuildResult> {
   319→    build_package(pkg_name, None, Some("release"))
   320→}
   321→
   322→/// Generate a temporary build.rs with scoped linker flags from tspec.toml
   323→pub fn generate_build_rs(path: &Path, crate_name: &str, spec: &Spec) -> Result<()> {
   324→    let mut lines = vec![TSPEC_BUILD_RS_MARKER.to_string(), "fn main() {".to_string()];
   325→
   326→    for arg in &spec.linker.args {
   327→        lines.push(format!(
   328→            "    println!(\"cargo:rustc-link-arg-bin={}={}\");",
   329→            crate_name, arg
   330→        ));
   331→    }
   332→
   333→    lines.push("}".to_string());
   334→    fs::write(path, lines.join("\n")).context("failed to write build.rs")?;
   335→    Ok(())
   336→}
   337→
   338→/// Cargo operation mode
   339→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   340→pub enum CargoMode {
   341→    Build,
   342→    Test,
   343→}
   344→
   345→impl CargoMode {
   346→    fn subcommand(&self) -> &'static str {
   347→        match self {
   348→            CargoMode::Build => "build",
   349→            CargoMode::Test => "test",
   350→        }
   351→    }
   352→}
   353→
   354→/// Check if spec requires nightly toolchain.
   355→/// For Test mode, panic=abort also needs nightly because `-Zpanic_abort_tests` is nightly-only.
   356→fn requires_nightly(spec: &Spec, mode: CargoMode) -> bool {
   357→    let panic_needs_nightly = match mode {
   358→        CargoMode::Test => spec
   359→            .panic
   360→            .map(|p| p.rustc_panic_value().is_some())
   361→            .unwrap_or(false),
   362→        CargoMode::Build => spec.panic.map(|p| p.requires_nightly()).unwrap_or(false),
   363→    };
   364→
   365→    let has_build_std = !spec.cargo.build_std.is_empty();
   366→    let has_unstable = !spec.cargo.unstable.is_empty();
   367→
   368→    panic_needs_nightly || has_build_std || has_unstable
   369→}
   370→
   371→/// Check if spec uses an abort-like panic mode that needs `-Zpanic_abort_tests` for testing.
   372→fn needs_panic_abort_tests(spec: &Spec) -> bool {
   373→    spec.panic
   374→        .map(|p| p.rustc_panic_value().is_some())
   375→        .unwrap_or(false)
   376→}
   377→
   378→/// Build the base cargo command (with toolchain if needed)
   379→fn build_cargo_command(spec: &Spec, mode: CargoMode) -> Result<Command> {
   380→    let mut cmd = Command::new("cargo");
   381→
   382→    if let Some(tc) = &spec.toolchain {
   383→        cmd.arg(format!("+{}", tc));
   384→    } else if requires_nightly(spec, mode) {
   385→        cmd.arg("+nightly");
   386→    }
   387→
   388→    Ok(cmd)
   389→}
   390→
   391→/// Apply spec parameters to a cargo command.
   392→/// `cli_profile` is the CLI-specified profile (None = debug default).
   393→pub fn apply_spec_to_command(
   394→    cmd: &mut Command,
   395→    spec: &Spec,
   396→    workspace: &Path,
   397→    cli_profile: Option<&str>,
   398→    expanded_target_dir: Option<&str>,
   399→) -> Result<()> {
   400→    // Set custom target directory if specified
   401→    if let Some(td) = expanded_target_dir {
   402→        cmd.arg("--target-dir")
   403→            .arg(workspace.join("target").join(td));
   404→    }
   405→
   406→    // Handle high-level panic mode (cargo -Z flag)
   407→    if let Some(panic_mode) = spec.panic
   408→        && let Some(z_flag) = panic_mode.cargo_z_flag()
   409→    {
   410→        cmd.arg("-Z").arg(z_flag);
   411→    }
   412→
   413→    // Handle cargo config
   414→    let has_profile = spec.cargo.profile.is_some();
   415→    if let Some(ref profile) = spec.cargo.profile {
   416→        match profile.as_str() {
   417→            "debug" | "dev" => {
   418→                // Debug/dev is default, no flag needed
   419→            }
   420→            _ => {
   421→                cmd.arg("--profile").arg(profile);
   422→            }
   423→        }
   424→    }
   425→
   426→    if let Some(ref triple) = spec.cargo.target_triple {
   427→        cmd.arg("--target").arg(triple);
   428→    }
   429→
   430→    if let Some(ref path) = spec.cargo.target_json {
   431→        cmd.arg("-Z").arg("json-target-spec");
   432→        cmd.arg("--target").arg(path);
   433→    }
   434→
   435→    for flag in &spec.cargo.unstable {
   436→        cmd.arg("-Z").arg(flag);
   437→    }
   438→
   439→    // Inject --config key=value pairs (flattened from potentially nested config)
   440→    for (key, value) in flatten_config(&spec.cargo.config) {
   441→        cmd.arg("--config").arg(format!("{}={}", key, value));
   442→    }
   443→
   444→    // If no profile in spec, fall back to CLI profile
   445→    if !has_profile && let Some(p) = cli_profile {
   446→        match p {
   447→            "debug" | "dev" => {} // default, no flag needed
   448→            _ => {
   449→                cmd.arg("--profile").arg(p);
   450→            }
   451→        }
   452→    }
   453→
   454→    // Collect rustc flags
   455→    let mut rustc_flags: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   456→
   457→    // Handle high-level panic mode (rustc -C flag)
   458→    if let Some(panic_mode) = spec.panic
   459→        && let Some(panic_value) = panic_mode.rustc_panic_value()
   460→    {
   461→        rustc_flags.push(format!("-C panic={}", panic_value));
   462→    }
   463→
   464→    // Handle high-level strip mode
   465→    if let Some(strip_mode) = spec.strip
   466→        && let Some(strip_value) = strip_mode.rustc_strip_value()
   467→    {
   468→        rustc_flags.push(format!("-C strip={}", strip_value));
   469→    }
   470→
   471→    if !spec.cargo.build_std.is_empty() {
   472→        // -Z build-std is a cargo flag, not rustc
   473→        let crates_str = spec.cargo.build_std.join(",");
   474→        cmd.arg("-Z").arg(format!("build-std={}", crates_str));
   475→    }
   476→
   477→    for flag in &spec.rustflags {
   478→        rustc_flags.push(flag.clone());
   479→    }
   480→
   481→    // Handle version script (generates file and adds linker arg)
   482→    if let Some(vs) = &spec.linker.version_script {
   483→        let vs_dir = match expanded_target_dir {
   484→            Some(td) => workspace.join("target").join(td),
   485→            None => workspace.join("target"),
   486→        };
   487→        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(&vs_dir);
   488→        let version_script_path = vs_dir.join("tspec-version.script");
   489→
   490→        // Generate version script: { global: sym1; sym2; local: *; };
   491→        let globals = vs.global.join("; ");
   492→        let content = format!("{{ global: {}; local: {}; }};", globals, vs.local);
   493→
   494→        let mut f =
   495→            fs::File::create(&version_script_path).context("failed to create version script")?;
   496→        writeln!(f, "{}", content)?;
   497→
   498→        rustc_flags.push(format!(
   499→            "-C link-arg=-Wl,--version-script={}",
   500→            version_script_path.display()
   501→        ));
   502→    }
   503→
   504→    // Apply rustc flags (linker args from Args handled by generated build.rs)
   505→    if !rustc_flags.is_empty() {
   506→        cmd.env("RUSTFLAGS", rustc_flags.join(" "));
   507→    }
   508→
   509→    Ok(())
   510→}
   511→

   629→    fn config_emits_config_args() {
   630→        use crate::types::ConfigValue;

   632→
   633→        let mut spec = Spec::default();
   634→        spec.cargo.config = BTreeMap::from([
   635→            (
   636→                "profile.release.opt-level".to_string(),
   637→                ConfigValue::String("s".to_string()),
   638→            ),
   639→            ("profile.release.lto".to_string(), ConfigValue::Bool(true)),
   640→            (
   641→                "profile.release.codegen-units".to_string(),
   642→                ConfigValue::Integer(1),
   643→            ),
   644→        ]);
   645→
   646→        let mut cmd = Command::new("cargo");
   647→        cmd.arg("build");
   648→        let workspace = PathBuf::from("/tmp/fake");

   650→
   651→        let args: Vec<String> = cmd.get_args().map(|a| a.to_string_lossy().into()).collect();
   652→
   653→        // BTreeMap is sorted, so codegen-units < lto < opt-level
   654→        let config_positions: Vec<usize> = args
   655→            .iter()
   656→            .enumerate()
   657→            .filter(|(_, a)| *a == "--config")
   658→            .map(|(i, _)| i)
   659→            .collect();
   660→        assert_eq!(config_positions.len(), 3);

   662→        // Check each --config arg

   664→            args[config_positions[0] + 1],

   666→        );
   667→        assert_eq!(args[config_positions[1] + 1], "profile.release.lto=true");
   668→        assert_eq!(

   670→            "profile.release.opt-level=\"s\""

    11→
    12→pub use build::BuildCmd;
    13→pub use clean::CleanCmd;
    14→pub use clippy::ClippyCmd;
    15→pub use compare::CompareCmd;
    16→pub use fmt::FmtCmd;

    23→use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};
    24→use std::ffi::OsString;
    25→use std::path::Path;
    26→use std::process::ExitCode;
    27→
    28→use crate::find_paths::{find_package_dir, find_project_root, get_package_name, is_pop};
    29→
    30→/// Trait for command execution.
    31→pub trait Execute {
    32→    fn execute(&self, project_root: &Path) -> Result<ExitCode>;
    33→}
    34→
    35→/// Resolve a `-p` argument (path or name) to the actual cargo package name.
    36→/// Returns Some(name) for a package, None if it resolves to a workspace root
    37→/// with no `[package]` section (meaning "operate on all packages").
    38→pub(crate) fn resolve_package_arg(pkg: &str) -> Result<Option<String>> {
    39→    let workspace = find_project_root()?;
    40→    let pkg_dir = find_package_dir(&workspace, pkg)?;
    41→    match get_package_name(&pkg_dir) {
    42→        Ok(name) => Ok(Some(name)),
    43→        Err(_) => Ok(None),
    44→    }
    45→}
    46→
    47→/// Helper for commands that execute a cargo subcommand.
    48→pub fn execute_cargo_subcommand(
    49→    subcommand: &str,
    50→    args: &[OsString],
    51→    project_root: &Path,
    52→) -> Result<ExitCode> {
    53→    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("cargo");
    54→    cmd.arg(subcommand);
    55→    cmd.args(args);
    56→    cmd.current_dir(project_root);
    57→    let status = cmd
    58→        .status()
    59→        .with_context(|| format!("failed to run cargo {}", subcommand))?;
    60→    if status.success() {
    61→        Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)
    62→    } else {
    63→        bail!("cargo {} failed", subcommand);
    64→    }
    65→}
    66→
    67→/// Check if current directory is a package (has Cargo.toml with [package]).
    68→/// Returns None at a workspace root so that commands default to all-packages mode.
    69→pub(crate) fn current_package_name() -> Option<String> {
    70→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
    71→    // At a workspace root, don't treat it as a single package
    72→    if cwd.join("Cargo.toml").exists() && !is_pop(&cwd) {
    73→        return None;
    74→    }
    75→    get_package_name(&cwd).ok()
    76→}
    77→

     1→use crate::cmd::{
     2→    BuildCmd, CleanCmd, ClippyCmd, CompareCmd, FmtCmd, InstallCmd, RunCmd, TestCmd, TsCmd,
     3→    VersionCmd,
     4→};
     5→use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
     6→
     7→#[derive(Parser)]
     8→#[command(name = "tspec", version, about = "Translation spec based build system")]
     9→#[command(before_help = concat!("tspec ", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")))]
    10→pub struct Cli {
    11→    #[command(subcommand)]
    12→    pub command: Commands,
    13→}
    14→
    15→#[derive(Subcommand)]
    16→pub enum Commands {
    17→    /// Build package(s) with a translation spec
    18→    Build(BuildCmd),
    19→    /// Build and run package(s) with a translation spec
    20→    Run(RunCmd),
    21→    /// Test package(s) with a translation spec
    22→    Test(TestCmd),
    23→    /// Clean build artifacts
    24→    Clean(CleanCmd),
    25→    /// Run clippy lints
    26→    Clippy(ClippyCmd),
    27→    /// Format source code
    28→    Fmt(FmtCmd),
    29→    /// Compare specs for a package (size only)
    30→    Compare(CompareCmd),
    31→    /// Manage translation specs
    32→    Ts(TsCmd),
    33→    /// Print version information
    34→    Version(VersionCmd),

{

  "old_string": "use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::fmt;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\nuse crate::options::{PanicMode, StripMode};",
  "new_string": "use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse std::collections::BTreeMap;\nuse std::fmt;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\nuse crate::options::{PanicMode, StripMode};\n\n/// Verbosity level for command output.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]\npub enum Verbosity {\n    #[default]\n    Normal,\n    /// -v: print command line + env vars\n    Verbose,\n    /// -vv: also print spec resolution details\n    Debug,\n}\n\nimpl Verbosity {\n    pub fn from_count(count: u8) -> Self {\n        match count {\n            0 => Verbosity::Normal,\n            1 => Verbosity::Verbose,\n            _ => Verbosity::Debug,\n        }\n    }\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/src/main.rs",
  "content": "use clap::Parser;\nuse std::process::ExitCode;\n\nuse tspec::cli::{Cli, Commands};\nuse tspec::cmd::Execute;\nuse tspec::find_paths::find_project_root;\nuse tspec::types::Verbosity;\n\nfn main() -> Result<ExitCode, anyhow::Error> {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let verbosity = Verbosity::from_count(cli.verbose);\n\n    match cli.command {\n        Commands::Build(cmd) => {\n            return cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity);\n        }\n        Commands::Run(cmd) => {\n            return cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity);\n        }\n        Commands::Test(cmd) => {\n            return cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity);\n        }\n        Commands::Clean(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Clippy(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Fmt(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Compare(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Ts(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Version(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n        Commands::Install(cmd) => {\n            cmd.execute(&find_project_root()?, verbosity)?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(ExitCode::SUCCESS)\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "pub fn run_cargo(\n    mode: CargoMode,\n    pkg_name: &str,\n    tspec: Option<&str>,\n    cli_profile: Option<&str>,\n) -> Result<BuildResult> {\n    let workspace = find_project_root()?;\n    let pkg_dir = find_package_dir(&workspace, pkg_name)?;\n    let tspec_path = find_tspec(&pkg_dir, tspec)?;\n\n    // Resolve actual package name from Cargo.toml (needed when pkg_name is a path)\n    let pkg_name = get_package_name(&pkg_dir)?;\n\n    // Clean up any stale tspec-generated build.rs from interrupted builds\n    let had_stale_build_rs = remove_stale_tspec_build_rs(&pkg_dir);\n\n    // Track if we generated a build.rs\n    let build_rs_path = pkg_dir.join(\"build.rs\");\n    let had_build_rs = build_rs_path.exists();\n\n    // Load spec once (if present) and compute target_dir\n    let (spec, expanded_td) = if let Some(path) = &tspec_path {\n        let s = load_spec(path)?;\n        let name = spec_name_from_path(path);\n        let td = expand_target_dir(&s, &name)?;\n        (Some(s), td)\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Compute paths\n    let target_base = match &expanded_td {\n        Some(td) => workspace.join(\"target\").join(td),\n        None => workspace.join(\"target\"),\n    };\n    let binary_path = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        get_binary_path(\n            &workspace,\n            &pkg_name,\n            spec,\n            cli_profile,\n            expanded_td.as_deref(),\n        )\n    } else {\n        get_binary_path_simple(&workspace, &pkg_name, cli_profile)\n    };\n\n    // Validate the effective profile exists before invoking cargo\n    let effective_profile = spec\n        .as_ref()\n        .and_then(|s| s.cargo.profile.as_deref())\n        .or(cli_profile);\n    if let Some(profile) = effective_profile {\n        validate_profile(profile, &workspace)?;\n    }\n\n    // Check for misconfigurations before running cargo\n    let spec_warnings = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        check_spec_misconfigurations(&pkg_name, spec, &pkg_dir)\n    } else {\n        Vec::new()\n    };\n\n    let verb = match mode {\n        CargoMode::Build => \"Building\",\n        CargoMode::Test => \"Testing\",\n    };\n\n    // Apply spec if present, otherwise plain cargo subcommand\n    let status = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        let path = tspec_path.as_ref().unwrap();\n        println!(\"{} {} with spec {}\", verb, pkg_name, path.display());\n\n        // Generate temporary build.rs for linker flags if needed\n        let has_linker_args = !spec.linker.args.is_empty();\n        let has_bin_target = pkg_dir.join(\"src/main.rs\").exists();\n        if has_linker_args && has_bin_target && !had_build_rs {\n            generate_build_rs(&build_rs_path, &pkg_name, spec)?;\n        }\n\n        let mut cmd = build_cargo_command(spec, mode)?;\n        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());\n        cmd.arg(\"-p\").arg(&pkg_name);\n        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);\n\n        // Set spec path for tspec-build library to read in build.rs\n        cmd.env(\"TSPEC_SPEC_FILE\", path.as_os_str());\n\n        apply_spec_to_command(\n            &mut cmd,\n            spec,\n            &workspace,\n            cli_profile,\n            expanded_td.as_deref(),\n        )?;\n\n        // For test mode, append -Zpanic_abort_tests to RUSTFLAGS if needed\n        if mode == CargoMode::Test && needs_panic_abort_tests(spec) {\n            let existing = cmd\n                .get_envs()\n                .find(|(k, _)| k == &\"RUSTFLAGS\")\n                .and_then(|(_, v)| v)\n                .map(|v| v.to_string_lossy().into_owned())\n                .unwrap_or_default();\n            let new_flags = if existing.is_empty() {\n                \"-Zpanic_abort_tests\".to_string()\n            } else {\n                format!(\"{} -Zpanic_abort_tests\", existing)\n            };\n            cmd.env(\"RUSTFLAGS\", new_flags);\n        }\n\n        cmd.status()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to run cargo {}\", mode.subcommand()))?\n    } else {\n        println!(\"{} {} (no tspec)\", verb, pkg_name);\n        let mut cmd = Command::new(\"cargo\");\n        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());\n        cmd.arg(\"-p\").arg(&pkg_name);\n        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);\n        if let Some(p) = cli_profile {\n            match p {\n                \"debug\" | \"dev\" => {}\n                _ => {\n                    cmd.arg(\"--profile\").arg(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        cmd.status()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to run cargo {}\", mode.subcommand()))?\n    };",
  "new_string": "pub fn run_cargo(\n    mode: CargoMode,\n    pkg_name: &str,\n    tspec: Option<&str>,\n    cli_profile: Option<&str>,\n    verbosity: Verbosity,\n) -> Result<BuildResult> {\n    let workspace = find_project_root()?;\n    let pkg_dir = find_package_dir(&workspace, pkg_name)?;\n    let tspec_path = find_tspec(&pkg_dir, tspec)?;\n\n    // Resolve actual package name from Cargo.toml (needed when pkg_name is a path)\n    let pkg_name = get_package_name(&pkg_dir)?;\n\n    // Clean up any stale tspec-generated build.rs from interrupted builds\n    let had_stale_build_rs = remove_stale_tspec_build_rs(&pkg_dir);\n\n    // Track if we generated a build.rs\n    let build_rs_path = pkg_dir.join(\"build.rs\");\n    let had_build_rs = build_rs_path.exists();\n\n    // Load spec once (if present) and compute target_dir\n    let (spec, expanded_td) = if let Some(path) = &tspec_path {\n        let s = load_spec(path)?;\n        let name = spec_name_from_path(path);\n        let td = expand_target_dir(&s, &name)?;\n        (Some(s), td)\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Compute paths\n    let target_base = match &expanded_td {\n        Some(td) => workspace.join(\"target\").join(td),\n        None => workspace.join(\"target\"),\n    };\n    let binary_path = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        get_binary_path(\n            &workspace,\n            &pkg_name,\n            spec,\n            cli_profile,\n            expanded_td.as_deref(),\n        )\n    } else {\n        get_binary_path_simple(&workspace, &pkg_name, cli_profile)\n    };\n\n    // Validate the effective profile exists before invoking cargo\n    let effective_profile = spec\n        .as_ref()\n        .and_then(|s| s.cargo.profile.as_deref())\n        .or(cli_profile);\n    if let Some(profile) = effective_profile {\n        validate_profile(profile, &workspace)?;\n    }\n\n    // Check for misconfigurations before running cargo\n    let spec_warnings = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        check_spec_misconfigurations(&pkg_name, spec, &pkg_dir)\n    } else {\n        Vec::new()\n    };\n\n    let verb = match mode {\n        CargoMode::Build => \"Building\",\n        CargoMode::Test => \"Testing\",\n    };\n\n    // Apply spec if present, otherwise plain cargo subcommand\n    let status = if let Some(spec) = &spec {\n        let path = tspec_path.as_ref().unwrap();\n        println!(\"{} {} with spec {}\", verb, pkg_name, path.display());\n\n        // -vv: print spec resolution details\n        if verbosity >= Verbosity::Debug {\n            let rel_path = path.strip_prefix(&workspace).unwrap_or(path);\n            println!(\"[debug] spec: {}\", rel_path.display());\n            let tc = if let Some(tc) = &spec.toolchain {\n                format!(\"{} (from spec)\", tc)\n            } else if requires_nightly(spec, mode) {\n                \"nightly (auto-detected)\".to_string()\n            } else {\n                \"default\".to_string()\n            };\n            println!(\"[debug] toolchain: {}\", tc);\n            if let Some(td) = &expanded_td {\n                println!(\"[debug] target_dir: {} (expanded from spec)\", td);\n            }\n            let eff = effective_profile.unwrap_or(\"debug\");\n            let source = if spec.cargo.profile.is_some() {\n                \"from spec\"\n            } else if cli_profile.is_some() {\n                \"from CLI\"\n            } else {\n                \"default\"\n            };\n            println!(\"[debug] effective profile: {} ({})\", eff, source);\n        }\n\n        // Generate temporary build.rs for linker flags if needed\n        let has_linker_args = !spec.linker.args.is_empty();\n        let has_bin_target = pkg_dir.join(\"src/main.rs\").exists();\n        if has_linker_args && has_bin_target && !had_build_rs {\n            generate_build_rs(&build_rs_path, &pkg_name, spec)?;\n        }\n\n        let mut cmd = build_cargo_command(spec, mode)?;\n        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());\n        cmd.arg(\"-p\").arg(&pkg_name);\n        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);\n\n        // Set spec path for tspec-build library to read in build.rs\n        cmd.env(\"TSPEC_SPEC_FILE\", path.as_os_str());\n\n        apply_spec_to_command(\n            &mut cmd,\n            spec,\n            &workspace,\n            cli_profile,\n            expanded_td.as_deref(),\n        )?;\n\n        // For test mode, append -Zpanic_abort_tests to RUSTFLAGS if needed\n        if mode == CargoMode::Test && needs_panic_abort_tests(spec) {\n            let existing = cmd\n                .get_envs()\n                .find(|(k, _)| k == &\"RUSTFLAGS\")\n                .and_then(|(_, v)| v)\n                .map(|v| v.to_string_lossy().into_owned())\n                .unwrap_or_default();\n            let new_flags = if existing.is_empty() {\n                \"-Zpanic_abort_tests\".to_string()\n            } else {\n                format!(\"{} -Zpanic_abort_tests\", existing)\n            };\n            cmd.env(\"RUSTFLAGS\", new_flags);\n        }\n\n        // -v: print command line and env vars\n        print_verbose_command(&cmd, verbosity);\n\n        cmd.status()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to run cargo {}\", mode.subcommand()))?\n    } else {\n        println!(\"{} {} (no tspec)\", verb, pkg_name);\n        let mut cmd = Command::new(\"cargo\");\n        cmd.arg(mode.subcommand());\n        cmd.arg(\"-p\").arg(&pkg_name);\n        cmd.current_dir(&workspace);\n        if let Some(p) = cli_profile {\n            match p {\n                \"debug\" | \"dev\" => {}\n                _ => {\n                    cmd.arg(\"--profile\").arg(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // -v: print command line even without spec\n        print_verbose_command(&cmd, verbosity);\n\n        cmd.status()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to run cargo {}\", mode.subcommand()))?\n    };"
}

  "new_string": "/// Print command details at verbose level.\nfn print_verbose_command(cmd: &Command, verbosity: Verbosity) {\n    if verbosity >= Verbosity::Verbose {\n        let prog = cmd.get_program().to_string_lossy();\n        let args: Vec<String> = cmd.get_args().map(|a| a.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).collect();\n        println!(\"[verbose] {} {}\", prog, args.join(\" \"));\n        for (key, val) in cmd.get_envs() {\n            if let Some(v) = val {\n                println!(\"[verbose] {}={}\", key.to_string_lossy(), v.to_string_lossy());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Build the base cargo command (with toolchain if needed)\nfn build_cargo_command(spec: &Spec, mode: CargoMode) -> Result<Command> {"
}

> AGENT

Committed 3f2f4d5.

Remember to commit .claude/ session files.

**Summary of Commit 3:** Added `--verbose`/`-v`/`-vv` global flag via `Verbosity` enum. Changed the `Execute` trait to accept `Verbosity`. Threaded it through all commands, `run_cargo()`, `build_package()`, `test_package()`, `plain_cargo_build_release()`, all batch `_all()` functions, and `compare_specs()`. At `-v`, prints the full cargo command line and env vars (`RUSTFLAGS`, `TSPEC_SPEC_FILE`). At `-vv`, also prints spec resolution details (spec path, toolchain source, target_dir, effective profile with source). 22 files changed, +188/-70.
